💡 COUNTER LEARNED — a line of ducks, walking in perfect formation. In the dark. That is not normal duck behavior.: Break the line. The line is one animal, but only while it's whole. 💡 COUNTER LEARNED — Nostalgia: Keep moving during the spell. The Home Movies beam needs your EYES — keep moving during the SPELL beat and the picture can't hold you.
